[\[139a.1\]]zhes bya ba ni/_gnyis po 'jig rten pa dang nyan thos dag go/_/mchog ces bya ba ni khyad par du 'phags pa'o/_/'bras bu'i sgo nas rigs khyad par du [#]bya bar tshigs su bcad pa/_yon tan shin tu rgya che byang chub shing bskyed phyir/_/bde dang sdug bsngal chen po zhi ba thob bya'i

[\[141a.1\]]dang 'dra bar bstan pa ste/_/de phyir de ltar yon tan phyug_/sems ni dga' bas yang dag bskyed/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sems bskyed pa dang po ni sa dang mtshungs te/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang de'i tshogs 'phel ba'i rten du gyur pa'i

[\[141a.3\]]dang ldan pa ni me dang 'dra ste/_shing gi bye brag dag gis me bzhin du gong nas gong du khyad par du 'gro ba'i phyir ro/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa ni khyad par rtogs pa'i bsam pa'o/_/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang ldan pa ni gter chen po bzhin te/_zang zing gi longs spyod kyis sems 
[\[141a.4\]]can dpag tu med pa tshim par byed pa dang mi zad pa'i phyir ro/_/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang ldan pa ni rin po che'i 'byung gnas lta bu ste/_de las yon tan rin po che thams cad 'byung ba'i phyir ro/_/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang ldan pa ni rgya mtsho dang 'dra [#]ste/_nyam nga ba 'byung ba

[\[141a.6\]]pha rol tu phyin pa dang ldan pa ni sman gyi rgyal po lta bu ste/_nyon mongs pa dang shes bya'i sgrib pa'i nad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i phyir ro/_/tshad med pa dang ldan pa ni mdza' bo chen po dang 'dra ste/_gnas skabs thams cad du sems can thams cad yal bar mi 'dor ba'i phyir ro/_/mngon 
[\[141a.7\]]par shes pa dang ldan pa ni yid bzhin gyi nor bu dang 'dra ste/_ji ltar mos pa bzhin de'i 'bras bu 'byor pa'i phyir ro/_/bsdu ba'i dngos po dang ldan pa bzhi [#]ni nyi ma dang 'dra ste/_gdul ba'i lo tog yongs su smin par byed pa'i phyir ro/_/so so yang dag par rig pa dang ldan pa ni dri za'i 
[\[141b\]]
[\[141b.1\]]dbyangs snyan pa lta bu ste/_gdul bya 'dul bar [#]byed ba'i chos ston pa'i phyir ro/_/rton pa dang ldan pa ni rgyal po lta bu ste/_chud mi za ba'i rgyu yin pa'i phyir ro/_/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dang ldan pa ni mdzod lta bu ste/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs mang po'i

[\[141b.7\]]dang de yi thabs rnyed dang /_/dgongs chen don dang de nyid mchog mthong bas/_/bde ba de spangs zhi bar 'gro bar 'gyur/_/sems bskyed pa de spangs pa'i sems can rnams kyis bde ba rnam pa bzhi mi 'thob ste/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi gzhan gyi don sems pas bde ba gang yin pa dang /_
[\[142a\]]
[\[142a.1\]]gzhan gyi don gyi thabs rnyed pas bde ba gang yin pa dang /_dgongs pa chen po'i don mthong ba ste/_theg pa chen po'i mdo zab mo dgongs pa can gyi don rtogs pas bde ba gang yin pa dang /_de kho na nyid mchog gi don chos la bdag med pa mthong bas bde ba gang yin 
[\[142a.2\]]pa'o/_/ngan 'gro dang yongs su skyo bas 'jigs pa med pa'i phyir/_sems bskyed pa bsngags par tshigs su bcad pa/_blo ldan sems mchog skyes ma thag tu yang /_/mtha' yas nyes pa byed las sems rab bsdoms[#]/_/gnyis 'phel byed pas rtag tu dge ba dang /_/brtse ldan bde dang 
[\[142a.3\]]sdug bsngal dag gis dga'/_/byang chub sems dpa'i [#]sems kyi mchog de skyes ma thag tu sems can mtha' yas pa'i rten can gyi nyes pa byed pa las sems rab tu bsdams par 'gyur te/_de'i phyir 'di ngan 'gros 'jigs par mi 'gyur ro/_/de ni dge ba'i las dang snying brtse ba gnyis 
[\[142a.4\]]'phel bar byed pas rtag tu dge ba dang ldan ba dang /_snying brtse ba dang ldan par yang 'gyur te/_des na de dge ba dang ldan pa'i phyir/_bde bas kyang dga' la snying brtse ba dang ldan pa'i phyir gzhan gyi don byed pa'i rgyu mtshan can gyi sdug bsngal gyis kyang dga'o/_/de'i phyir 'di bya ba mang pos 
[\[142a.5\]]yongs su skyo bas kyang 'jigs par mi 'gyur ro/_/mi byed pa'i sdom pa thob par tshigs su bcad pa/_gang tshe gzhan gyi don phyir lus dang ni/_/srog la mi lta rab ngal khas len pa/_/de lta bu de gzhan gyi gnod pa yis/_/ji ltar ngan par byed pa'i las la 'jug_/de'i bsdus pa'i 
[\[142a.6\]]don ni/_gang zhig gzhan gyi don gyi phyir lus dang srog la yang mi lta ba'i phyir/_gzhan nyid ches sdug gi bdag nyid ni ma yin pa de ji ltar gzhan gyis gnod pa byas pas bdag gi [#]don du nyes par byed pa'i las la 'jug par 'gyur/_sems mi ldog par tshigs su bcad pa gnyis 
[\[142a.7\]]te/_/de yis chos kun sgyu ma lta bu dang /_/skye [#]ba skyed mos tshal 'gro ltar rtogs nas/_/'byor pa'i dus na rgud pa'i dus na'ang 'di/_/nyon mongs sdug bsngal dag gis 'jigs pa med/_/rang gi yon tan sems can phan 'dogs dga'/_/bsams [#]bzhin skye ba rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul/_/
[\[142b\]]
[\[142b.1\]]rgyan dang ston mo sa mchog rtse dga' ba/_/snying rje'i bdag nyid min pa rnams la med/_/byang chub sems dpa' des chos thams cad sgyu ma lta bur mthong ba'i phyir/_'byor pa'i dus na nyon mongs pa rnams kyis mi 'jigs la/_skye ba skyed mos tshal du 'gro ba lta bur mthong ba'i phyir/_
[\[142b.2\]]rgud pa'i dus na sdug bsngal gyis mi 'jigs na/_de'i byang chub kyi sems 'jigs pa gang gis ldog par 'gyur/_gzhan yang rang gi yon tan gyis [#]rgyan dang /_gzhan la phan 'dogs pas dga' ba'i ston mo dang /_bsams bzhin du skye ba'i skyed mos tshal gyi sa dang /_rdzu [#]
[\[142b.3\]]'phrul gyis rnam par 'phrul pa'i rtsed mos dga' ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dag kho na la yod kyi/_byang chub sems dpa' ma yin pa rnams la ni med na de dag gis [#]sems ji ltar zlog [#]par 'gyur/_sdug bsngal gyis 'jigs pa dgag par tshigs su bcad pa/_gzhan don brtson ldan snying 
[\[142b.4\]]rje'i bdag nyid 'di/_/gang tshe mnar med pa la'ang dga' [#]'dzin pa/_/de 'dra ba dag srid na gzhan rten can/_/sdug bsngal 'byung [#]ba rnams kyis ga la 'jigs/_/gzhan yang gzhan gyi don la brtson pa gang zhig snying rje'i bdag nyid can yin pa'i phyir/_mnar med pa la yang dga' bar 
[\[142b.5\]]'dzin pa de ji ltar srid pa na gzhan gyi don gyi rgyu mtshan can gyi sdug bsngal 'byung ba dag gis 'jigs par 'gyur te/_gang gi [#]phyir na 'di sdug bsngal gyis 'jigs pa'am sems ldog par 'gyur/_sems can yal bar 'dor ba [#]dgag par tshigs su bcad pa/_slob dpon brtse chen rtag tu 
[\[142b.6\]]bsten [#]pa'i bdag_/pha rol sdug bsngal dag gis sems gdung ba/_/gzhan gyi bya ba [#]nye bar gnas pa la/_/gzhan gyis [#]bskul ba shin tu ngo yang tsha/_/slob dpon snying rje rtag tu bsten pa'i bdag nyid can pha rol gyi sdug bsngal dag gis sems gdung ba gang yin pa de la gal te gzhan gyi 
[\[142b.7\]]don [#]bya ba byung ba la dge ba'i bshes gnyen gzhan dag gis bskul bar [#]bya dgos pa ni te bor ngo tsha ba'o[#]/_/le lo smad par tshigs su gcad [#]pa/_mgo la sems can khur chen khyer ba yi/_/sems can mchog ni dal gyis 'gro mi mdzes/_/bdag gzhan sna tshogs 'ching bas rab bcings pa/_/
[\[143a\]]
[\[143a.1\]]brtson pa brgya 'gyur du ni bya ba'i rigs/_/byang chub sems dpa' mgo la sems can gyi khur chen po thogs nas pha rol gnon pa dal ba ni mi [#]mdzes kyi/_des nyan thos kyi brtson 'grus pas brtson 'grus brgya 'gyur du bya bar rigs te/_'di ltar de ni bdag 
[\[143a.2\]]dang gzhan gyi 'ching ba rnam pa sna tshogs nyon mongs pa dang las dang skye ba'i ngo bo dag gis shin tu bcings pa yin no/_/sems bskyed pa'i skabs rdzogs so//_//sgrub pa'i mtshan nyid kyi tshigs su bcad pa/_gnas rtsom 'bras bu 'byung ba che bdag nyid/_/rgyal sras 
[\[143a.3\]]rnams kyi sgrub pa yin par 'dod/_/rtag tu len pa che dang bzod pa che/_/don chen yang dag sgrub pa'i bya ba byed/_/de la gnas chen po ni sems bskyed pa la gnas pa'i phyir ro/_/rtsom pa chen po ni rang dang gzhan gyi don rtsom pa'i phyir ro/_/'bras bu 'byung ba chen po ni 'bras bu byang chub 
[\[143a.4\]]chen po 'thob pa yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de nyid kyi phyir [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du len pa che ba ste/_sems can thams cad nye bar len pa'i phyir ro/_/bzod pa che ba ste/_sdug bsngal thams cad dang du len pa'i phyir ro/_/don chen yang dag sgrub pa'i bya ba byed pa ste/_sems can mang po'i don 
[\[143a.5\]]yang dag par sgrub pa'i phyir ro/_/rang dang gzhan gyi don khyad par med par tshigs su bcad pa/_gzhan dang bdag tu mnyam pa'i sems nyid dam/_/bdag pas gzhan ches sdug pa rnyed nas ni/_/de ltar bdag pas gzhan don mchog tu shes/_/rang gi don gang gzhan gyi don gang 
[\[143a.6\]]yin/_/gzhan dang bdag mnyam pa'i sems nyid brda las byung ba'i sems bskyed pa rnyed pa'i tshe mos pa'i sgo nas sam/_don dam [#]pa'i sems bskyed pa rnyed pa'i tshe/_shes pa'i sgo nas rnyed nas sam/_yang na bdag pas gzhan ches shin tu sdug bsngal ba rnyed nas rgyu de nyid kyis 
[\[143a.7\]]bdag pas gzhan gyi [#]don la khyad par 'phags par 'du shes pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i [#]la rang gi don ni gang zhig yin la gzhan gyi don gang zhig yin te/_de la gnyis ka khyad par med do zhes bya ba'i tha tshig go/_/gzhan gyi don khyad par du bya bar tshigs su bcad pa/_ji ltar snying 
[\[143b\]]
[\[143b.1\]]rje'i bdag nyid gzhan gyi don gyi [#]phyir/_/bdag la sdug bsngal mi bzad gdung ba rab byed pa/_/de ltar 'jig rten gdung ba'i las kyis gzhan dgra la/_/shin tu brtse ba med par rab tu 'jug ma yin/_/ji ltar 'di'i gzhan gyi don bdag nyid kyi don las khyad par du 'phags pa de sgrub 
[\[143b.2\]]par byed de/_gzhan gyi don du bdag nyid la shin tu gdung bar byed pa'i phyir ro/_/gzhan gyi don sgrub par rnam par dbye bar tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_tha dad [#]'bring dang mchog chos nyid gnas la/_/legs par ston dang mgu byed 'jug pa dang /_/don la rnam nges dge la [#]
[\[143b.3\]]yongs smin byed/_/de bzhin gdams ngag gnas dang blo rnams grol/_/yon tan khyad par can 'grub de bzhin du/_/rigs su skye dang lung bstan dbang bskur dang /_/de bzhin gshegs mkhyen bla med go 'phang ste/_/bcu gsum bdag nyid 'di ni gzhan gyi don/_/sems can 
[\[143b.4\]]gyi ris rnam pa gsum po rigs dman pa dang 'bring dang khyad par can la gnas pa la byang chub sems dpa'i gzhan gyi don rnam pa bcu gsum ste/_rjes su bstan pa dang /_kun brjod pa'i cho 'phrul dag gis legs par ston pa dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul gyis mgu bar byed pa dang /_bstan 
[\[143b.5\]]pa khas len du gzhug pa'i phyir 'jug pa dang /_zhugs pa rnams kyi [#]the tshom gcod pa'i phyir don la [#]rnam par nges par byed pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smin par byed pa dang /_'doms pa dang /_sems gnas par byed pa dang /_shes rab rnam par grol bar byed pa dang /_mngon bar shes 
[\[143b.6\]]pa la sogs pa yon tan khyad par can rnams yang dag par 'grub par byed pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skye ba dang /_sa brgyad pa la lung bstan pa dang /_sa bcu pa la dbang bskur ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes dang ldan pa'o/_/rnam pa bcu [#]gsum po 'di dag 
[\[143b.7\]]ni ci rigs par rigs la gnas pa gsum byang chub sems dpa'i don [#]yin no/_/gzhan gyi don sgrub pa phun sum tshogs par tshigs su bcad pa/_skye bo dang mthun phyin ci ma log 'chad/_/mi 'gying ba dang bdag gi [#]med la mkhas/_/bzod dul shin tu ring song mi bzad [#]pa/_/rgyal sras 
[\[144a\]]
[\[144a.1\]]rnams kyi sgrub pa mchog yin no/_/'dis ni gzhan gyi don sgrub pa 'di phun sum tshogs pa yin pa de ston to/_/'di ji ltar phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_gal te rigs la gnas pa'i skye bo dang mthun zhing /_phyin ci ma log [#]par 'chad par 'gyur ba dang /_'gying ba 
[\[144a.2\]]med par mgu bar 'gyur ba dang /_mi mthun pa mi byed pas bdag gi ba med par 'jug pa ste/_rdzu 'phrul gyis mi rlom zhing sems can zhugs pa rnams la bdag gi bar 'dzin pa med pa dang /_don rnam par nges par bya ba la mkhas pa'i sgrub pa yin te/_dge ba yongs su smin par bya ba la 
[\[144a.3\]]bzod pa'i sgrub pa yin/_dul bas 'doms pa la sogs pa'i sgrub pa yin te/_ma dul bas ni gzhan dag la gdams pa la sogs bstan par mi nus so/_/rigs su skyes pa la sogs pa shin tu ring du song ba'i sgrub pa yin no/_/ring du ma song ba'i sgrub pas ni/_gzhan dag rigs su 
[\[144a.4\]]skyes pa la sogs par [#]bya bar mi nus so/_/byang chub sems dpa'i sgrub pa 'di dag thams cad kyang khas blangs pa'i sems can mi zad pa'i phyir mi zad pa yin te/_de'i phyir yang phun sum tshogs pa yin par rig par bya'o/_/sgrub pa khyad par [#]du bya bar tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_
[\[144a.5\]]'dod ldan skye bo 'jigs pa che la 'jug_/srid dga' phyin ci log bde g.yo ba la/_/zhi dga' rang gi len par rab zhi la/_/snying rje'i bdag nyid len kun zhi la rtag[# ]/skye bo rmongs pa rang gi bde don brtson/_/rtag tu de ma thob par sdug bsngal 'gyur/_/brtan pa rtag tu gzhan don 
[\[144a.6\]]brtson pa ni/_/gnyi [#]ga'i don byas mya ngan 'da' bar 'gyur/_/de la 'dod pa rnams 'jigs pa chen po nyid yin pa ni lus dang sems kyi sdug bsngal mang po dang /_ngan 'gror 'gro ba'i rgyu yin pa'i phyir ro/_/phyin ci log gi bde ba g.yo ba ni gzugs dang gzugs med pa'i srid 
[\[144a.7\]]pa'i dga' ba rnams mi rtag pa nyid kyi phyir dang /_'du byed kyi sdug bsngal gyis don dam par sdug bsngal ba'i phyir ro/_/len pa dag ni nyon mongs pa rnams su rig par bya ste/_sdug bsngal len pa'i phyir ro/_/skye bo rmongs pa rtag tu rang bde ba'i don du zhugs pa ni bde ba mi 
[\[144b\]]
[\[144b.1\]]'thob kyi/_sdug bsngal kho na 'thob bo/_/byang chub sems dpa' gzhan gyi [#]don du zhugs pa ni/_rang dang gzhan gyi [#]don bsgrubs nas mya ngan las 'das pa'i bde ba 'thob ste/_'di ni sgrub pa'i khyad par gzhan yin no/_/spyod yul yongs su bsngo [#]bar tshigs su bcad pa/_rgyal sras 
[\[144b.2\]]spyod tshe ji lta ji lta bur/_/dbang po'i spyod yul sna tshogs 'jug 'gyur ba/_/de lta de ltar rigs shing mthun tshig [#]gis/_/sems can phan phyir de mngon 'du byed do/_/byang chub sems dpa' spyod lam byed pa'i spyod pa la 'jug pa na rnam pa gang dang gang gis mig la sogs pa'i dbang po'i 
[\[144b.3\]]spyod yul sna tshogs la 'jug pa'i rnam pa de dang der 'brel zhing 'dra ba'i tshig gis sems can rnams la phan par bya ba'i phyir de thams cad ji ltar spyod yul yongs su dag pa'i mdo las rgyas par bstan pa bzhin mngon par 'du byed do/_/sems can rnams la mi bzod pa dgag par 
[\[144b.4\]]tshigs su bcad pa/_rtag tu nyes pas rang dbang med par byas sems kyis[#]/_/skye bo la ni blo ldan nyes par mi 'dzin te/_/mi 'dod bzhin du log par sgrub par 'gyur ro zhes/_/skye bo dag la snying rje rnam par 'phel phyir ro/_/rtag tu nyon mongs pa rnams kyis rang dbang med par byas pa'i 
[\[144b.5\]]sems dang ldan pa'i skye bo la byang chub sems dpa' nyes par mi 'dzin to/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di dag ni mi 'dod bzhin du log par sgrub pa dag tu 'gyur ro zhes bya bar rig nas snying rje 'phel bar 'gyur ba'i phyir ro/_/sgrub pa'i che ba nyid kyi tshigs su bcad pa/_sgrub pa 'di ni srid dang 
[\[144b.6\]]'gro ba kun/_/zil gyis mnan 'gro zhi ba mchog dang ldan/_/yon tan sna tshogs tshogs kyis [#]rnam par 'phel/_/byams pa'i bsam pas [#]rtag tu 'gro ba sdud/_/che ba nyid ni rnam pa bzhi ste/_zil gyis [#]gnon pa'i che ba nyid ni/_srid pa gsum dang 'gro ba lnga po thams cad zil gyis 
[\[144b.7\]]mnan nas 'gro ba'i phyir te/_ji skad du/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las/_rab 'byor gal te gzugs dngos por 'gyur gyi[#]/_dngos po med pa ma [#]yin na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'dis lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten zil [#]gyis mnan nas nges par 'byung bar mi 
[\[145a\]]
[\[145a.1\]]'gyur zhes bya ba la sogs pa rgyas par gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/mya ngan las 'das pa'i che ba nyid ni mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/yon tan gyi che ba nyid dang /_sems can yongs su mi gtong ba'i che ba nyid ni yon tan gyis 'phel ba 
[\[145a.2\]]pa dang /_'gro ba sdud pa'i phyir ro/_/sgrub pa'i skabs rdzogs so//_//don dam pa'i mtshan nyid du tshigs su bcad pa/_yod min med min de bzhin min gzhan min/_/skye dang 'jig med 'bri bar mi 'gyur te/_/'phel ba med cing rnam par dag pa'ang med/_rnam par dag 
[\[145a.3\]]'gyur de ni don dam mtshan/_/gnyis med pa'i don ni don dam pa ste/_gnyis med pa'i don de yang rnam pa lngas yang dag par ston to/_/kun brtags pa dang gzhan gyi [#]dbang gi mtshan nyid kyis ni yod pa ma yin no/_/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid [#]kyis ni med pa ma yin no/_/kun 
[\[145a.4\]]brtags pa dang gzhan gyi [#]dbang dag dang yongs su grub par gcig pa nyid med pas de bzhin ma yin no/_/de dag kho na dang gzhan pa nyid med pas gzhan ma yin no/_/chos kyi dbyings 'dus ma byas pa'i phyir skye ba yang med/_'jig [#]pa yang med do/_/kun nas nyon mongs pa dang 
[\[145a.5\]]rnam par byang ba dag 'gag pa dang skye ba dang [#]de bzhin gnas pa'i phyir 'bri bar yang mi 'gyur/_'phel bar yang mi 'gyur ro/_/rang bzhin gyis kun nas nyon mongs pa can ma yin pa'i phyir/_rnam par dag par 'gyur ba med do/_/glo bur gyi nyon mongs pa dang bral bas [#]rnam par ma dag 
[\[145a.6\]]pa yang ma yin te/_de ltar na gnyis med pa'i mtshan nyid rnam pa lnga po de ni don dam pa'i mtshan nyid yin par rig par bya'o/_/bdag tu lta ba'i phyin ci log dgag par tshigs su bcad pa/_bdag tu lta ba bdag nyid bdag mtshan min/_/ngan par gnas min mtshan nyid mi 'dra phyir/_/
[\[145a.7\]]gnyis las gzhan med de ni nor pa [#]skyes/_/de phyir thar pa nor tsam zad pa yin/_/re zhig bdag tu lta ba nyid ni bdag gi mtshan nyid ma yin no/_/ngan par gnas pa nyid kyang ma yin te/_'di ltar de ni bdag gi mtshan nyid du kun brtags pa'i bdag dang mtshan nyid mi 'dra ba'i phyir ro/_/de 
[\[145b\]]
[\[145b.1\]]yang nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga ste/_nyon mongs pa'i gnas ngan len gyis rab tu bskyed pa'i phyir ro/_/'di gnyis las gzhan pa'i bdag gi mtshan nyid kyang mi dmigs te/_de lta bas na bdag ni med kyi bdag tu lta ba gang yin pa de ni nor pa skyes pa yin no/_/bdag med pa nyid kyi phyir/_thar 
[\[145b.2\]]pa yang nor pa tsam du [#]zad pa yin par rig par bya'i grol ba ni 'ga' yang med do/_/phyin ci log smad par tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_ji ltar 'khrul pa tsam brten [#]skye bo yis/_/sdug bsngal rang bzhin rgyun chags rtogs mi 'gyur/_/rigs min rig nyid sdug bsngal sdug bsngal 
[\[145b.3\]]med/_/chos kyi rang bzhin de yi rang bzhin min/_/brten nas dngos rab skye bar mngon 'jug pa'i/_/skye bo ji ltar gzhan gyis byas la brten[#]/_/gang phyir yod pa mi mthong med mthong ba[#]/_/'di 'dra'i mun nag rnam pa de gang zhig_/ji ltar na 'jig rten 'di dag [#]tu lta ba nor pa tsam la brten [#]
[\[145b.4\]]nas 'du byed rnams kyi [#]sdug bsngal gyis ngo bo nyid rtag tu rjes su 'brel pa mi mthong ste/_sdug bsngal gyi [#]rang bzhin de ni ye shes kyis rig par bya mi dgos te/_sdug bsngal nyams su myong bas rig pa yin no/_/sdug bsngal ma spangs pas sdug bsngal bar gyur pa yin la 
[\[145b.5\]]sdug bsngal dang ldan pa'i bdag med pa'i phyir sdug bsngal bar gyur pa med do/_/gang zag [#]la bdag med pas chos tsam du zad pa'i phyir chos kyi rang bzhin yin no/_/chos la bdag med pas chos kyi rang bzhin ma yin no/_/yang gang gi [#]tshe 'jig rten gyis de dang de la brten nas dngos 
[\[145b.6\]]po de dang de dag 'byung ngo zhes bya bar dngos po rnams kyi rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba mngon sum du mthong ba na de ji ltar mthong ba la sogs pa ni gzhan gyis byas kyi[#]/_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba ni ma yin no [#]zhes bya ba'i [#]lta ba 'di la brten/_'jig rten gyi [#]mun nag gang rten cing 
[\[145b.7\]]'brel par 'byung ba yod pa ni mi mthong gi_/med pa bdag tu rtogs pa 'di 'dra ba de gang zhig yin te/_yod pa ni mun pas mi mthong bar nus kyi/_med pa mthong bar ni ma yin no/_/bdag med [#]par zhi ba rung bar tshigs su bcad pa/_'di la don dam tshul gyis zhi ba dang /_/
[\[146a\]]
[\[146a.1\]]skye ba de gnyis khyad par ci yang med/_/'on kyang dge ba'i las rnams byed pa dag_/skye ba zad pas zhi ba 'thob par bshad/_/don dam pa'i tshul bdag med par mnyam pa nyid kyis 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa dag la tha dad du bya ba ci yang med do/_/
[\[146a.2\]]'on kyang gang dag thar pa'i lam sgom [#]pa dge ba'i las byed pa dag gi skye ba zad pas thar pa 'thob par 'gyur ba nyid do/_/phyin ci log smad pa byas nas de'i gnyen po don dam pa'i ye shes la 'jug par tshigs su bcad pa bzhi ste/_byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams ye 
[\[146a.3\]]shes kyi/_/mtha' yas pha rol tshogs rnams legs bsags nas/_/chos la sems pa shin tu rnam nges phyir/_/don gyi [#]rnam pa brjod pa'i rgyu can rtogs/_/de yis brjod pa tsam du don rig nas/_/der snang sems tsam la ni yang dag gnas/_/de nas chos dbyings 
[\[146a.4\]]gnyis kyi mtshan nyid dang /_/bral ba mngon sum nyid du rtogs par 'gyur/_/sems las gzhan med par ni blos rig [#]nas/_/de nas sems kyang med pa nyid du rtogs/_/blo dang ldan pas gnyis po med rig nas/_/de mi ldan pa'i chos kyi dbyings la gnas/_/blo dang ldan 
[\[146a.5\]]pa'i mi rtog [#]ye shes stobs/_/rtag tu kun nas mnyam par song ba yis/_/de la brten pa'i nyes tshogs tshang tshing dag_/dug sman chen pos dug bzhin sel bar byed/_/gcig gis ni tshogs chen po bsags pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin la brten nas bsam [#]pa'i phyir chos 
[\[146a.6\]]bsam pa nyid du [#]rnam par nges pa nyid dang /_yid kyi brjod pa yin pa'i phyir chos de dag don du snang bar rtogs pa'i phyir de la 'jug pa rab tu ston te[#]/_dus rab tu dbye ba grangs med pas 'di'i pha rol yongs su rdzogs pas [#]na mtha' yas pha rol yin no/_/gnyis pas ni don rnams 
[\[146a.7\]]yid kyis [#]brjod pa tsam du rig nas der snang ba'i sems tsam la gnas pa ston te/_'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa'i gnas skabs yin no/_/de phan chad ni chos kyi dbyings gnyis kyi mtshan nyid gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa'i mtshan nyid dang bral ba mngon 
[\[146b\]]
[\[146b.1\]]sum nyid du rtogs te/_'di ni mthong ba'i lam gyi skabs yin no/_/gsum pas ni ji ltar 'dis chos kyi dbyings mngon sum nyid du rtogs pa ston to/_/ji ltar 'dis mngon sum du rtogs she na/_sems las gzhan pa'i dmigs pa gzung [#]ba med do zhes bya bar blos rtogs nas gzung 
[\[146b.2\]]ba med na 'dzin pa yang med pa'i phyir sems tsam po de yang med pa nyid du khong du chud do/_/de gnyis med pa nyid du rig nas de dang mi ldan pa'i chos kyi [#]dbyings gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa'i mtshan nyid dag dang bral ba la gnas te/_de ltar na chos kyi dbyings mngon sum nyid du rtogs pa 
[\[146b.3\]]yin no/_/bzhi pas ni bsgom [#]pa'i lam gyi [#]gnas skabs na gnas yongs su gyur pas don dam pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa ston te/_rnam par mi rtog pa'i ye shes kyi stobs kyis dus kun du mnyam pa nyid du song bas de la brten pa'i nyes pa'i tshogs ring du rjes su zhugs pa/_gnas ngan 
[\[146b.4\]]len gyi mtshan nyid dag dug sman chen pos dug bzhin du sel ba'i phyir ro/_/de gang la mnyam pa nyid du song zhe na/_gzhan gyi dbang gi ngo bo nyid la'o/_/don dam pa'i ye shes kyi che ba nyid kyi tshigs su bcad pa/_thub pas gsungs pa'i dam chos legs bzhag pa/_/rtsa ba'i chos 
[\[146b.5\]]kyi dbyings la blo gros bzhag_/dran rgyu rtog pa tsam rig brtan [#]pa dag_/myur du yon tan rgya mtsho'i pha rol 'gro/_/byang chub sems dpa' don dam pa'i ye shes la zhugs pa des [#]sangs rgyas kyis gsungs pa'i [#]dam pa'i chos legs par bzhag pa'i rtsa ba'i sems bsdus pa'i chos 
[\[146b.6\]]la dmigs pa'i chos kyi dbyings la blo gros bzhag nas dran pa gang dang gang skye ba'i dran pa 'jug pa de dag thams cad rtog pa tsam du khong du chud de/_de ltar na myur du yon tan rgya mtsho'i pha rol sangs rgyas nyid du 'gro bar [#]'gyur bas 'di ni don dam pa'i ye shes kyi che ba nyid yin no/_/de [#]
[\[146b.7\]]kho na nyid kyi skabs rdzogs so//_//mthu'i mtshan nyid rnam par dbye bas tshigs su bcad pa/_skye [#]dang ngag sems dge dang mi dge bskyed/_/de yi gnas dang 'byung ba mngon sum gang /_/shes pa kun 'gro rab tu dbye bcas la/_/thogs med bstan pa thob pa'i mthu yin no/_/gzhan

[\[147a.1\]]dag gi skye ba shes pa ni/_'chi 'pho dang skye ba mngon par shes pa yin no/_/de dang de dag tu skyes pa rnams ci smra ba'i tshig shes pa ni/_lha'i rna ba'i mngon par shes pa yin no/_/sems shes pa ni sems kyi rnam grangs mngon par shes pa yin no/_/sngon

[\[151a.1\]]bsam pa de ston par byed de/_phan pa dang bde ba la yang dag par sbyor ba'i phyir/_pha ma dang gnyen bshes kyi bsam pa las khyad par du 'phags pa dang /_'jig rten bdag la byams pa las khyad par du 'phags pa'i bsam pa yin no/_/'jig rten bdag la

[\[156a.4\]]'od zer gcig byung na 'od zer kun 'byung [#]ba/_de bzhin sangs rgyas rnams kyi yang /_/ye shes 'byung bar shes par bya/_/dus gcig tu 'od zer thams cad 'byung ba ltar sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ye shes dus gcig tu 'jug par ston to/_/ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od zer dag_/
[\[156a.5\]]'jug la bdag gir bya ba med/_/de bzhin sangs rgyas ye shes kyang /_/'jug la bdag gir mdzad mi mnga'/_/nyi ma'i 'od zer gcig btang bas/_/'gro 'di snang bar byed pa ltar/_/de bzhin shes bya thams cad la/_/sangs rgyas ye shes cig car snang /_/bdag gir bya 
[\[156a.6\]]ba med pa dang /_/shes bya cig car [#]snang bar byed pa la [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_don brda phrad par zad do/_/ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od zer rnams/_/sprin la sogs pas bsgribs [#]par 'dod/_/de bzhin sangs rgyas ye shes rnams/_/sems can gyi [#]ni

[\[156b\]]
[\[156b.1\]]gos la tshon bkra mi bkra ba/_/de bzhin 'phen pa'i dbang gis na/_/grol ba'i ye shes bkra mi bkra/_/ji ltar mdud pa'i khyad par gyis gos la kha cig tu ni tshon khra bo nyid du 'gyur la/_kha cig tu ni khra bo nyid du mi 'gyur ba de bzhin du sngon gyi smon lam gyi spyod pa'i stobs bskyed pa'i khyad

[\[156b.5\]]la sogs pa rin po che'i gnas nyid kyi phrin las dang /_sems can yongs su smin par mdzad pa'i phrin las dang /_mthar thug par gshegs pa'i phrin las dang /_chos ston pa'i phrin las dang /_sprul pa la sogs pa mdzad pa'i phrin las dang /_ye shes [#]'jug pa'i phrin las dang /_rnam par mi rtog pa'i phrin

[\[157a.1\]]gshegs pa yang de rnam par dag pa'i ngo bo nyid yin te/_de'i phyir sems can thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po can zhes bya ba 'byor pa rnam par dbye ba la tshigs su bcad pa bcu gcig go/_/nyan thos rnams kyi 'byor pas ni/_/'jig rten pa yi zil gyis gnon/_/

[\[160a.1\]]rten dang dgongs pa dang /_/phrin las kyis ni mnyam pa nyid/_/rang bzhin dang ni rgyun mi 'chad/_/rgyun gyis de dag rtag pa nyid/_/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku gsum po de dag ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du rnam pa gsum gyis khyad par med de/_rten gyis ni chos kyi dbyings tha mi

[\[160b\]]
[\[160b.1\]]yin pa'i phyir ye shes thams cad kyi 'byung gnas lta bu'o/_/de ni longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyang yin la/_ye shes kyi gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i phyir/_me long lta bu'i ye shes zhes kyang bya'o/_/sgom pa dag pas sems can la/_/mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes 'dod/_/mi gnas zhi 
[\[160b.2\]]bar zhugs pa ni/_/mnyam nyid ye shes yin par 'dod/_/byang chub sems dpas [#]mngon par rtogs pa'i dus na sems can rnams la mnyam pa nyid kyi shes pa thob pa gang yin pa de sgom pa dag pa'i sgo nas byang chub thob pa'i dus na [#]mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das par zhugs pa ni mnyam pa nyid 
[\[160b.3\]]kyi ye shes su 'dod do/_/dus rnams kun tu byams pa dang /_/thugs rje chen po dag dang ldan/_/sems can rnams la mos pa bzhin/_/sangs rgyas sku ni nges par ston/_/dus thams cad du byams pa chen po dang /_thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa dang /_sems can rnams la mos pa ji lta ba 
[\[160b.4\]]bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku nges par ston par mdzad pa yin te/_'di ltar sems can kha cig gis ni de bzhin gshegs pa kha dog sngon por/_kha cig gis ni kha dog ser por mthong ngo zhes bya ba de lta bu la sogs pa'o/_/so sor rtog [#]pa'i ye shes ni/_/shes bya kun la rtag mi thogs/_/
[\[160b.5\]]ting nge 'dzin dang gzungs rnams kyi/_/gter dang 'dra ba kho na yin/_/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rnams su ni/_/'byor pa thams cad ston mdzad pa/_/the tshom thams cad gcod pa yi/_/chos chen char ni rab tu 'bebs/_/so sor rtog [#]pa'i ye shes ni tshigs su bcad pa ji lta ba bzhin no/_/
[\[160b.6\]]bya ba sgrub pa'i ye shes ni/_/khams rnams kun tu sna tshogs shing /_/dpag med bsam yas sprul pa yis/_/sems can don ni sgrub pa'o/_/bya ba sgrub [#]pa'i ye shes ni 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du sprul pa rnam pa sna tshogs dpag tu med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag gis sems 
[\[160b.7\]]can thams cad kyi don mdzad pa'o/_/sangs rgyas sprul pa de rtag tu/_/mdzad pa grub [#]pa'i bye brag dang /_/grangs dang zhing gis rnam kun tu/_/bsam mi khyab par shes par bya/_/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa de ni rtag tu bya ba mdzad pa'i bye brag dang grangs dang zhing gi sgo nas rnam pa thams cad du bsam 
[\[161a\]]
[\[161a.1\]]gyis mi khyab par rig par bya'o/_/'dzin pa'i phyir dang sems mnyam phyir/_/yang dag chos rab ston phyir dang /_/bya ba sgrub pa nyid kyi phyir/_/ye shes bzhi po yang dag 'byung /_/de la 'dzin pa'i phyir ni thos pa'i chos rnams la'o/_/sems mnyam pa'i phyir ni sems 
[\[161a.2\]]can thams cad la bdag dang gzhan mnyam pa nyid kyis so/_/lhag ma ni don go bar zad do/_/sangs rgyas gcig pa nyid dang /_du ma nyid ma yin pa'i tshigs su bcad pa/_tha dad rigs don yod phyir dang /_/rdzogs dang thog ma med pa'i phyir/_/sangs rgyas gcig min dri med gnas/_/tha 
[\[161a.3\]]dad min phyir mang po'ang min/_/sangs rgyas ni gcig kho na'o zhes bya ba 'di ni mi 'dod do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs tha dad pa'i phyir te/_sems can sangs rgyas kyi rigs can mtha' yas pa de la gcig kho na zhig mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas kyi/_gzhan dag ni mngon par 
[\[161a.4\]]rdzogs par 'tshang mi rgya'o zhes bya ba 'di ci'i phyir mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang mi rgya ba'i phyir/_byang chub sems dpa' gzhan gyi bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs don med par yang [#]'gyur ro/_/don med par ni rigs pa ma yin te/_de lta bas na don med pa ma yin pa'i phyir yang 
[\[161a.5\]]sangs rgyas gcig kho na ma yin no/_/su yang sangs rgyas nyid la mi 'god [#]pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi [#]sems can gyi don mdzad pa yang rdzogs par mi [#]'gyur te/_de ni rigs pa ma yin no/_/tshogs med par yang sangs rgyas nyid du mi rung la/_sangs rgyas gzhan med par yang tshogs mi 
[\[161a.6\]]rung ba'i phyir thog ma'i sangs rgyas 'ga' yang med pas thog ma med pa'i phyir yang sangs rgyas gcig tu rig [#]pa ma yin no/_/zag pa med pa'i dbyings na chos kyi sku la tha dad pa med pa'i phyir/_sangs rgyas rnams kyi sku mang po nyid du yang mi 'dod do/_/sangs rgyas nyid la 'jug pa'i/_
[\[161a.7\]]thabs kyi tshigs su bcad pa bzhi'o/_/med pa gang yin de nyid ni/_/yod pa yi ni dam pa yin/_/rnam pa kun tu mi dmigs pa/_/dmigs pa yi ni mchog tu 'dod/_/kun brtags pa'i ngo bo nyid du med pa gang yin pa de nyid yongs su grub pa'i ngo bo nyid du yod pa'i dam pa yin la/_
[\[161b\]]
[\[161b.1\]]kun brtags pa'i ngo bo nyid rnam pa thams cad du mi dmigs pa gang yin pa de nyid yongs su grub pa'i ngo bo nyid mi dmigs pa'i mchog yin no/_/sgom pa rnam par mi lta ba'i/_/sgom pa dam par 'dod pa ste/_/thob par lta ba med rnams kyi/_/thob pa yang ni dam par 'dod/_/sgom pa mi 
[\[161b.2\]]dmigs pa gang yin pa de nyid sgom pa dam pa yin la/_thob pa'i [#]mi dmigs pa gang yin pa de nyid thob pa dam pa yin no/_/bla ma ring dang mtshan ma dang [#]/_/bdag gis [#]brtson 'grus lta [#]ba yi/_/byang chub sems dpa' nga rgyal can/_/byang chub las ni ring zhes bya/_/gang dag sangs rgyas nyid 
[\[161b.3\]]la bla ma ya mtshan gyi chos dang ldan par lta ba dang /_de yang dag par 'grub par bya ba dus ring bar lta ba dang /_mtshan ma ste sems kyi [#]dmigs par lta ba dang /_bdag gis [#]brtson 'grus brtsams pa yin te/_sangs rgyas nyid thob par bya'o zhes bdag nyid kyi brtson 'grus su lta ba'i byang chub 
[\[161b.4\]]sems dpa' nga rgyal can de lta bu de dag ni dmigs pa can yin pa'i phyir/_byang chub las ring ba zhes bya'o/_/ji skad bshad pa 'di dag kun/_/rtog pa tsam du lta ba yi/_/byang chub sems dpa' rtogs [#]med rnams/_/byang chub thob ces nges par brjod/_/'di dag thams cad ni rtog pa 
[\[161b.5\]]tsam zhes bya bar lta ba rnams ni rtog pa tsam de la yang mi rtog pa'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rtog [#]pa med pa rnams ni mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob pa'i gnas skabs na don du byang chub 'thob pa dang 'dra bar nges par brjod do/_/sangs rgyas rnams phan tshun mdzad pa gcig pa nyid la tshigs su bcad 
[\[161b.6\]]pa bzhi'o/_/klung [#]dag sa yi 'og tu ma song ba/_/gnas rnams tha dad chu yang tha dad pa/_/chu yang chung zhing bya ba tha dad byed/_/chur [#]gnas srog chags phra mo'i nyer spyad 'gyur/_/rgya mtshor song bar gyur na thams cad kyang /_/gnas rnams gcig cing chu yang che la gcig_/bya ba gcig cing chu la 
[\[161b.7\]]gnas pa yi/_/srog chags mang po'i rtag tu nyer spyad [#]che/_/sangs rgyas nyid du ma zhugs brtan [#]pa rnams/_/rten kyang tha dad blo gros tha dad cing /_/rtogs chung rang gi bya ba tha dad la/_/sems can nyung don rtag tu nyer spyad 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas nyid du zhugs na de dag kun/_/rten kyang gcig 
[\[162a\]]
[\[162a.1\]]cing rtogs pa che la gcig_/mdzad pa gcig tu 'dres shing rtag par yang /_/sems can tshogs che'i nye bar spyad pa che/_/de la klung gi gnas tha dad [#]pa ni rang gi snod tha dad pa'i phyir ro/_/bya ba byed pa tha dad pa ni bya ba tha dad pa nyid du byed pa'i phyir ro/_/phra mo'i nye bar spyad pa 
[\[162a.2\]]zhes bya ba ni/_/chung ngu rnams kyi nye bar spyad pa'o/_/lhag ma ni don go bar zad do/_/sangs rgyas nyid la spro bar bya ba'i [#]tshigs su bcad pa/_de ltar sangs rgyas nyid ni dkar po'i chos/_/dpe med ldan phyir phan bde'i rgyu yi phyir/_/bde dge dam pa mi zad 'byung gnas phyir/_/blo ldan byang 
[\[162a.3\]]chub sems dge blang ba'i rigs/_/de ltar sangs rgyas nyid ni rang gi don phun sum tshogs pas dkar po'i chos dpe med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir dang /_gzhan gyi don phun sum tshogs pas phan pa dang bde ba'i rgyu yin pa'i phyir dang /_bde bar gnas pa'i khyad par gyis bde ba kha na ma tho ba 
[\[162a.4\]]med pa mchog zad [#]mi shes pa'i 'byung gnas yin pa'i phyir blo dang ldan pas der smon pa yongs su gzung ba'i sgo nas byang chub kyi sems blang ba'i [#]rigs so/_/byang chub kyi skabs rdzogs so//_//sdom ni dang po sgrub skyabs rigs/_/de bzhin du ni sems bskyed dang /_/rang gzhan 
[\[162a.5\]]don dang de nyid don/_/mthu dang yongs smin byang chub bo/_/byang chub kyi skabs 'di dang po nas bzung ste/_ril gyis byang chub kyi le'u'i rjes su 'brangs te [#]sbyar bar khong du chud par bya'o/_/mos pa rnam par dbye ba la tshigs su bcad pa gnyis so/_/skye [#]dang ma skyes 'dzin dang gzung 
[\[162a.6\]]gyur dang /_/grogs blangs bdag nyid dang ni nor pa dang /_/ma nor gzhan dang mngon sum ma yin gzhan/_/sgras byung tshol ba dang ni lta ba pa'o[#]/_/skyes pa ni 'das pa dang da ltar byung ba'o/_/ma skyes pa ni ma 'ongs pa'o/_/'dzin pa ni nang gi ste/_gang gis dmigs pa la mos par byed pa'o/_/
[\[162a.7\]]gzung bar gyur pa ni/_phyi rol gyi sde gang la dmigs pa nyid du mos pa'o/_/grogs po las blangs pa ni rags pa'o/_/bdag nyid las byung [#]ba ni phra ba'o/_/nor pa ni dman pa ste log par mos pa'i phyir ro/_/ma nor bar ni gya nom pa'o/_/mngon sum pa ni nye ba ste/_/rkyen nye ba'i phyir ro/_/mngon 
[\[162b\]]
[\[162b.1\]]sum ma yin pa ni ring ba ste bzlog pa'o/_/sgra las byung ba ni thos pa las byung ba'o/_/tshol ba pa ni bsags [#]pa las byung ba'o/_/lta ba pa [#]ni bsgoms pa las byung ba ste so sor rtog pa'i phyir ro/_/'phrogs dang 'dres dang mi mthun phyogs ma 'dres/_/dman dang rgya che bsgribs [#]dang ma bsgribs dang /_/
[\[162b.2\]]brtson dang ma [#]brtson bsags dang ma bsags pa/_/brtan [#]zhugs ring du song ba'i mos pa'o/_/'phrogs pa ni chung ngu'o/_/'dres pa ni 'bring po'o/_/mi mthun pa'i phyogs rnams dang ma 'dres pa ni chen po'o/_/dman pa ni theg pa gzhan la'o/_/rgya chen po ni theg pa chen po la'o/_/bsgribs pa 
[\[162b.3\]]ni khyad par du 'gro bar bya ba la sgrib pa dang bcas pa'o/_/ma bsgribs [#]pa ni sgrib pa med pa'o/_/brtson pa ni rtag tu dang gus par byas te sbyor ba'i phyir ro/_/mi brtson pa ni de dag dang bral ba'o/_/bsags pa ni rtog [#]par rung ba'o/_/ma bsags pa ni bzlog pa'o/_/brtan [#]par zhugs pa ni sa 
[\[162b.4\]]la zhugs pa'o/_/ring du song ba ni sa lhag ma rnams la'o/_/mos pa'i bar du gcod pa la tshigs su bcad pa gsum mo/_/yid la mi byed mang ba dang /_/le lo rnal 'byor 'khrugs pa dang /_/grogs ngan dge ba zhan pa dang /_/tshul bzhin ma yin yid la byed/_/skyes pa la ni yid la mi byed 
[\[162b.5\]]pa mang ba bar du gcod do/_/ma skyes pa la ni le lo'o/_/gzung ba dang 'dzin par gyur [#]pa la ni rnal 'byor 'khrugs pa ste/_de kho na bzhin du [#]mngon par zhen pa'i phyir ro/_/grogs po las blangs pa ni/_grogs po ngan pa ste log par 'dzin du 'jug pa'i phyir ro/_/bdag nyid mos pa la ni dge ba'i 
[\[162b.6\]]rtsa ba nyam chung ba'o/_/ma nor ba la ni [#]tshul bzhin ma yin pa yid la byed pa bar du gcod de/_de dang 'gal ba'i phyir ro/_/bag med pa dang thos pa nyung /_/thos dang bsam pa nyung ngus chog[# ]/zhi ba tsam gyis [#]nga rgyal dang /_/de bzhin ma 'dris yin par 'dod/_/mngon sum pa la ni bag 
[\[162b.7\]]med pa ste/_de ni bag yod pas byas pa'i phyir ro/_/sgra las byung ba ni thos pa nyung ba ste/_nges pa'i don gyi mdo sde ma thos pa'i phyir ro/_/tshol ba pa la ni thos pa tsam gyis [#]chog par 'dzin pa dang bsam pa nyung ngus chog par 'dzin pa'o/_/lta ba pa la ni bsam pa tsam gyis chog [#]par 'dzin pa 
[\[163a\]]
[\[163a.1\]]dang /_zhi gnas tsam gyis mngon pa'i nga rgyal byed pa'o/_/'phrogs pa dang /_'dres pa dag la ni ma 'dres pa bar du gcod do/_/mi skyo ba dang de bzhin skyo/_/sgrib pa dang ni mi brtson pa/_/tshogs ma bsags pa mos pa yi/_/bar du gcod par shes par bya/_/dman pa 
[\[163a.2\]]la ni 'khor ba las mi skyo ba'o/_/rgya chen po la ni skyo ba'o/_/ma bsgribs pa la ni sgrib pa'o/_/brtson pa la ni mi brtson pa'o/_/bsags pa la ni ma bsags pa bar du gcod do/_/mos pa'i phan yon la tshigs su bcad pa lnga ste/_bsod nams che dang 'gyod pa med/_/yid bde ba dang bde ba 
[\[163a.3\]]che/_/chud mi za dang brtan [#]pa dang /_/de bzhin khyad par 'gro ba dang /_/chos ni mngon par rtogs pa dang /_/bdag dang gzhan don thob [#]pa'i mchog_/mngon shes myur ba de dag ni/_/mos pa yi [#]ni phan yon yin/_/skyes pa da ltar byung ba'i ni bsod nams chen po'o/_/'das pa'i ni 'gyod pa 
[\[163a.4\]]med pa ste/_yid la gcags pa med pa'i phyir ro/_/'dzin pa dang gzung bar gyur pa'i ni yid bde ba chen po ste/_ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/dge ba'i grogs pos bskyed pa'i ni chud mi za'o[#]/_/bdag nyid mos pa'i ni brtan [#]pa'o/_/ma nor ba dang /_mngon du ba dang /_thos pa las 
[\[163a.5\]]byung ba la sogs pa nas 'bring gi bar gyi ni khyad par du 'gro ba'o/_/chen po'i ni chos mngon par rtogs pa'o/_/theg pa dman pa'i ni bdag gi don thob pa'o/_/rgya chen po'i ni gzhan gyi don thob pa'i mchog go/_/ma bsgribs pa dang /_brtson pa la sogs pa dkar po'i phyogs rnams kyi 
[\[163a.6\]]phan yon ni mngon par shes pa myur ba nyid do/_/'dod pa can ni [#]de khyi 'dra/_/ting nge [#]'dzin pa'i rus [#]sbal bzhin/_/rang don byed pa'i de bran 'dra/_/gzhan don byed pa'i rgyal po bzhin/_/ji ltar khyi ni rtag tu ltogs shing sdug bsngal gyis [#]nyen chog mi shes/_/ji ltar rus [#]sbal de ni chu 
[\[163a.7\]]yi nang na kun nas bskums shing 'dug_/ji ltar bran ni rtag tu 'jigs shing skrag pa'i lus kyis [#]rnam par rgyu/_/ji ltar rgyal po yang ni bka' yis yul la dbang bsgyur gnas pa ltar[#]/_/de bzhin g.yem dang gnas dang rang gzhan phan bya don byed pa'i/_/mos pa sna tshogs dag gis rtag 
[\[163b\]]
[\[163b.1\]]tu khyad par shes par bya/_/theg pa chen po de la de [#]mchog tshul bzhin rig nas ni/_/'di na brtan pas de la [#]de nyid rtag par rab tu tshol/_/yang 'dod pa can rnams kyi mos pa ni khyi dang 'dra'o/_/'jig rten pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pa rnams kyi ni rus [#]sbal bzhin no/_/rang gi don dang ldan 
[\[163b.2\]]pa rnams kyi ni bran dang 'dra'o/_/gzhan gyi don dang ldan pa rnams kyi ni rgyal po bzhin no/_/don de nyid gzhan gyis bstan nas/_gzhan gyis [#]theg pa chen po la mos pa yang dag par len du 'jug pa yin no/_/mos pa la zhum pa dgag par tshigs su bcad pa/_gang phyir sems can mir 
[\[163b.3\]]gyur pa/_/tshad med skad cig re re la/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob de'i phyir/_/zhum pa dang du blang mi bya/_/rgyu gsum gyis zhum par rigs pa ma yin te/_'di ltar mir gyur pas 'thob pa dang /_dpag tu med pas 'thob pa dang /_rtag tu 'thob pa'o/_/mos pa'i bsod nams kyi khyad 
[\[163b.4\]]par la tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_gzhan dag la ni zas byin pa'i/_/bsod nams 'phel ba ji lta bar/_/bdag nyid za ba'i ma yin pa/_/de bzhin gzhan don rten gyi chos/_/bstan las mdo las gsungs pa yi/_/bsod nams cher 'byung thob pa ltar/_/rang gi don gyi rten gyi chos/_/
[\[163b.5\]]bstan las thob pa ma yin no/_/ji ltar gzhan gyi don gyi dbang du byas nas kha zas byin pa'i bsod nams skye bar 'gyur ba ltar/_rang gi don gyi dbang du byas nas bdag nyid za ba [#]ma yin pa de bzhin du gzhan gyi don gyi rten theg pa chen po'i chos bstan pa las[#]/_theg pa chen po'i mdo de dang de dag 
[\[163b.6\]]las gsungs pa'i bsod nams 'byung ba chen po 'thob pa ltar rang gi don gyi rten nyan thos kyi theg pa'i chos bstan pa las ni ma yin no/_/mos pa'i 'bras bu yongs su bsdu bar tshigs su bcad pa/_de ltar 'phags pa chen po'i chos ni yangs gyur la/_/blo dang ldan bas rtag tu mos pa che bskyed 
[\[163b.7\]]na/_/bsod nams rgya chen rgyun mi 'chad dang de 'phel dang /_/yon tan dpag tu med pas bdag nyid chen por 'gyur/_/gang la dang gang zhig dang mos pa ji lta bus 'bras bu gang [#]yongs su 'dzin pa ste/_theg pa chen po'i chos rgya chen po la blo gros dang ldan pas mos pa yongs su nyams par mi 
[\[164a\]]
[\[164a.1\]]'gyur ba rgya chen pos 'bras bu rnam pa gsum po bsod nams rgya chen po 'phel ba dang /_mos pa de nyid 'phel ba dang /_de'i rgyu las byung ba yon tan mtshungs pa med pas bdag nyid chen po sangs rgyas nyid yongs su 'dzin to/_/mos pa'i skabs rdzogs so//_//
[\[164a.2\]]chos yongs su tshol ba'i skabs las/_dmigs pa yongs su tshol ba la tshigs su bcad pa bzhi'o/_/sde snod dag gis [#]gsum mam gnyis kyang rung /_/bsdus pa'i phyir te rgyu ni dgus 'dod do/_/de ni bag chags rtogs dang zhi ba dang /_/rab tu rtogs pas rnam par grol bar byed/_/sde 
[\[164a.3\]]snod gsum ni/_mdo sde dang /_'dul ba dang /_chos mngon pa rnams so/_/gsum po de dag nyid theg pa dman pa dang /_theg pa mchog gi bye brag gis rnam pa [#]gnyis su 'gyur te/_nyan thos kyi sde snod dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod do/_/gsum po'am/_gnyis po de yang ci'i 
[\[164a.4\]]phyir sde snod ces bya zhe na/_smras pa/_bsdus pa'i phyir te/_shes bya'i don thams cad bsdus pa'i phyir ro/_/ci'i phyir gsum yin zhe na/_rgyu dgus te/_the tshom gyi gnyen por ni mdo sde ste/_gang zhig don gang la the tshom za ba de la de [#]nges par bya ba'i ched du bshad pa'i phyir ro/_/
[\[164a.5\]]mtha' gnyis la sbyor ba'i gnyen por ni 'dul ba ste/_kha na ma tho ba dang bcas pa'i yongs su spyad pa bkag pa'i sgo nas 'dod pa'i bsod nams la rjes su sbyor ba'i mtha' dang /_kha na ma tho ba med pa'i yongs su spyad pa rjes su gnang ba'i sgo nas bdag nyid dub pa la sbyor ba'i mtha'o[#]/_/rang 
[\[164a.6\]]gi lta ba mchog tu 'dzin pa'i gnyen por ni chos mngon pa ste/_chos kyi mtshan nyid phyin ci ma log par ston pa'i phyir ro/_/yang mdo sdes ni bslab pa gsum bshad do/_/'dul bas ni lhag pa'i tshul khrims dang /_lhag pa'i sems sgrub pa yin te/_tshul khrims dang ldan pa ni 'gyod 
[\[164a.7\]]pa med pa la sogs pa'i rim gyis ting nge 'dzin 'thob pa'i phyir ro/_/chos mngon pa bas ni lhag pa'i shes rab sgrub [#]pa yin te/_don phyin ci ma log par rab tu 'byed pa'i phyir ro/_/yang mdo sdes ni chos dang don bshad do/_/'dul bas ni chos dang don sgrub pa ste/_nyon mongs pa 
[\[164b\]]
[\[164b.1\]]'dul ba la rab tu brtson pas de gnyis rtogs pa'i phyir ro/_/chos mngon pas ni chos dang don 'brel pa'i gtam rnam par gtan la dbab pa la mkhas pa ste/_rgyu dgu po 'dis [#]sde snod gsum du 'dod do/_/de yang 'khor ba las rnam par grol bar bya ba'i ched [#]yin te/_des ji ltar rnam 
[\[164b.2\]]par grol bar byed ce na/_des ni bag chags rtogs dang zhi ba dang /_/rab tu rtogs pas [#]rnam par grol bar byed/_/thos pas ni sems la bag chags kyi sgo nas so/_/bsams [#]pas ni rtogs par byed pa'i sgo nas so/_/bsgoms pas ni zhi gnas kyis zhi bar byed pa'i sgo nas [#]dang /_lhag 
[\[164b.3\]]mthong gis rab tu rtogs pa'i sgo nas so/_/mdo dang chos mngon 'dul ba dag_/mdor na don ni rnam bzhir 'dod/_/blo dang ldan pas de shes nas/_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa thob[#]/_/mdo sde dang /_chos mngon pa dang /_'dul ba de dag ni mdor bsdu [#]na/_so so re re zhing don 
[\[164b.4\]]rnam pa bzhi bzhi ste/_byang chub sems dpas de rnams shes nas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid 'thob bo/_/nyan thos kyis ni tshigs su bcad pa gcig gi don shes pas kyang zag pa zad pa 'thob bo/_/gnas rnams dang ni mtshan nyid dang /_/chos don smos phyir mdo yin no/_/mngon

[\[192a.6\]]dgas so zhes gsungs pa ni snang ba 'di yin no/_/de chos kyi snang ba de [#]nyid spel ba'i phyir mi gnas pa'i byed pas brtson 'grus brtan po rtsom par byed pa de ni 'di'i rtse mo'i gnas skabs yin no/_/de [#]chos kyi snang ba 'phel nas sems tsam [#]la gnas te/_'di ni sems kho nar zad do 
[\[192a.7\]]zhes bya bar rtogs [#]pa'i phyir ro/_/de nas don du snang ba thams cad ni sems kho nar zad kyi/_don sems las gzhan pa ni ma yin no zhes bya bar mthong ste/_de'i tshe ni [#]'di'i gzung ba'i rnam par g.yeng ba spangs la/_'dzin pa'i rnam par g.yeng ba 'ba' zhig lus 
[\[192b\]]
[\[192b.1\]]so/_/de ni 'di'i bzod pa'i gnas skabs yin no/_/de'i tshe bar chad med pa'i ting nge 'dzin la yang myur du reg par 'gyur te/_de ni 'di'i 'jig rten pa'i chos kyi mchog gi gnas skabs yin no/_/ci'i phyir de 'di'i bar chad med pa zhes bya zhe na/_'di ltar de yi mjug [#]
[\[192b.2\]]thogs su/_/'dzin pa'i rnam par g.yeng ba spang [#]/_/'di dag [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du ni/_/dro bar gyur pa yin zhes bya/_/de ltar na 'di dag ni nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun par dro bar gyur pa la sogs pa yin no/_/de nas des ni 'jig rten las/_/'das pa'i ye shes bla med

[\[192b.4\]]med pa ni mthong bas spang [#]bar bya ba'i nyon mongs pa spangs pa'i phyir te/_'dis ni rdul dang bral ba dang /_dri ma dang bral ba zhes bshad pa yin no/_/'dzin pa gnyis dang bral ba ni gzung ba dang 'dzin pa dang bral ba'i phyir ro/_/de 'di'i gnas yongs gyur pa ste/_/sa ni dang po

[\[192b.6\]]pa'i sems 'thob ste/_/sems can rnams la bdag med dang /_/sdug bsngal dang ni don bya dang /_/lan mi re la 'di sems snyoms/_/rgyal sras gzhan dang 'dra ba yin/_/chos la bdag med pas chos [#]mnyam pa nyid rtogs nas mtshungs pa nyid rnam pa lngas rtag tu sems

[\[193a\]]
[\[193a.1\]]ni rang dang gzhan gyi sdug bsngal spang bar [#]bya dgos par mtshungs pa'i phyir ro/_/lan mi re ba mtshungs pa nyid kyis ni bdag nyid las bzhin du gzhan las lan mngon par mi 'dod pa'i phyir ro/_/de las [#]gzhan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang mtshungs pa nyid

[\[193a.3\]]'dus byas rnams yang dag pa ma yin pa'i kun du rtog pa tsam du mthong ste/_ye shes shin tu rnam par dag pa ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i phyir ro/_/gnyis su med pa'i don gyis zhes bya ba ni gzung ba med pa dang /_'dzin pa med pa'i don gyis so/_/de dag med pa'i ngo bo ni/_/

[\[193b.1\]]yang dag pa ma yin pa'i kun tu rtog pa dmigs pa yin par shes par bya'o/_/rgyal sras byang chub phyogs mthun par[#]/_rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad ni/_/rtag tu mthong ba'i lam de dang /_lhan cig tu ni thob par 'dod/_/byang chub sems dpa'i byang chub kyi phyogs dang mthun

[\[219a.1\]]bya'o/_/zhes byang chub sems dpa' sbyin pa la rjes su ston par byed do/_/gal te khyod 'bras thob pa mi gtong na/_/sbyin la mngon dga' yin par mi 'gyur te/_/'di ltar skad cig tsam [#]yang sbyin med na/_/sbyin la mngon par dga' ba nyid ma yin/_/zhes bya ba [#]
[\[219a.2\]]ba'i tshigs su bcad pa ni don go bar zad do/_/ma byas pa la 'bras bu mi 'byin te/_/'di ltar lan bltas bdag gi cha ma yin/_/khyod ni gang zhig khyod byed pa de la 'bras bu 'byin te/_de lta bas na lan la lta bas bdag gi cha ma yin gyi/_'di ltar bdag ni/_khyod kyi 
[\[219a.3\]]lan la lta dang bral bas na/_/'bras bu shin tu gzhan la gtong ba yin/_/'di ni don go bar zad do/_/brtse bas sbyin pa'i tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_rgyal sras rnams kyi brtse bas sbyin pa ni/_/kha na ma tho med cing dag pa'i gzhi/_/bde ba 'dren par byed pa nyid 
[\[219a.4\]]dang ni/_/rjes su bsrung bcas tshol med gos pa med/_/de la kha na ma tho ba med pa ni gzhan la mi gnod par sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/gzhi dag pa ni dug dang mtshon dang chang la sogs pa spangs pa'i sgo nas dngos po rung ba sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/bde ba bskyed [#]pa ni/_sbyin pas kun tu 
[\[219a.5\]]bsdus nas dge ba la sbyor ba'i phyir ro/_/rjes su bsrung ba dang bcas pa ni 'khor gyi skye bo la mi gnod par gzhan la sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/tshol ba med pa ni ma bslangs kyang don du [#]gnyer ba nyid dam/_phongs par khong du chud nas bdag nyid kyis [#]sbyin pa'i phyir dang /_yon gnas 
[\[219a.6\]]mi 'dam pa'i phyir ro/_/gos pa med pa ni/_lan dang rnam par smin pa la dga' ba med pa'i phyir ro/_/rnam pa gzhan yang /_rgyal sras rnams [#]kyi brtse bas sbyin pa ni/_mtha' dag rgya che gtso dang rtag pa dang /_/dga' dang zang zing med dang dag pa dang /_/byang chub 
[\[219a.7\]]la gzhol dge la gzhol ba yin/_/de la mtha' dag ni nang dang [#]phyi'i dngos po sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/rgya chen po ni dngos po mang po sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/gtso bo ni dngos po gya nom pa sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/rtag pa ni yang dang yang du sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/dga' ba ni so sor brtags 
[\[219b\]]
[\[219b.1\]]pa med par rab tu dga' bas sbyin pa'i phyir ro/_/zang zing med pa ni gos pa med pa ji lta ba bzhin no/_/dag pa ni gzhi dag pa ji lta ba bzhin no/_/byang chub la gzhol ba ni byang chub chen por yongs su bsngos pa'i phyir ro/_/dge ba la gzhol ba ni bde ba bskyed [#]pa ji lta ba bzhin no/_/nye 
[\[219b.2\]]bar spyod [#]pa'i khyad par gyi tshigs su bcad pa/_ji ltar bde ba gsum gyis tshim pa'i yid/_/brtse ldan yongs su gtong bas dga' 'gyur bas/_/longs spyod can dag nye bar spyod [#]pa yis/_/de ltar dga' bar 'gyur ba ma yin no/_/de la bde ba gsum ni sbyin pas dga' 
[\[219b.3\]]ba dang /_gzhan rjes su 'dzin pas dga' ba dang /_/byang chub kyi tshogs rdzogs pas dga' ba'o/_/lhag ma ni don go bar zad do/_/pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par sgrub pa'i snying rje'i tshigs su bcad pa/_/bkren la brtse dang ma rungs pa la brtse/_/kun 'khrugs la brtse bag med [#]
[\[219b.4\]]rnams la brtse/_/yul gyi gzhan dbang can la snying rje dang /_/log par mngon par zhen la snying rje ba'o/_/de la bkren pa ni ser sna can rnams so/_/ma rungs pa ni tshul khrims 'chal pa gzhan la gnod pa rnams so/_/kun tu 'khrugs pa ni khro ba can rnams so/_/bag med pa ni le lo can rnams so/_/yul gyi gzhan 
[\[219b.5\]]dbang can ni 'dod pa dag la sems rnam par g.yengs pa [#]rnams so/_/log par mngon par zhen pa ni/_shes rab 'chal ba mu stegs can la sogs pa'o/_/pha rol tu phyin pa'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs la gnas pa 'di dag la/_snying rje ba gang yin pa de ni bkren pa la sogs pa 
[\[219b.6\]]la snying rje ba yin te/_de yang de'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs rnam par sun 'byin pa'i phyir pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par sgrub par byed par 'gyur ro/_/de ltar na pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par sgrub pa'i snying rje zhes bya'o/_/snying rje'i rkyen bstan pa'i tshigs su bcad pa/_bde dang sdug 
[\[219b.7\]]bsngal de rgyu las/_/byang chub sems dpa'i snying rje ste/_/rgyu dang bshes gnyen rang bzhin las/_/byang chub sems dpa'i snying rje'o/_/de la phyed gong mas ni snying rje'i dmigs pa'i rkyen ston te/_tshor ba rnam pa gsum la dmigs nas sdug bsngal nyid gsum gyis snying rje bar 
[\[220a\]]
[\[220a.1\]]byed pa'i phyir ro/_/bde ba yang ma yin sdug bsngal ba yang ma yin pa'i tshor ba ni bde ba dang sdug bsngal dag gi rgyu yin te/_yang de dag 'dren pa'i phyir ro/_/phyed 'og mas ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du rgyu dang bshes gnyen dang rang bzhin dag gis snying rje'i rgyu dang bdag po dang 
[\[220a.2\]]mtshungs pa de ma thag pa'i rkyen rnams ston to/_/snying rje chen po nyid kyi tshigs su bcad pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i snying rje ni/_/mnyam par shes bya de bsam phyir/_/sgrub phyir 'dod chags bral phyir dang /_/mi dmigs phyir dang dag phyir ro/_/de la mnyam pa ni tshor ba rnam pa gsum 
[\[220a.3\]]car [#]gyi gnas skabs su gang ci tshor ba de ni [#]'dir sdug bsngal ba'o zhes bya bar rig pa'i phyir ro/_/de yang bsam pa'i phyir ni sems [(]gyis [,]kyis [)]snying rje ba'i phyir ro/_/sgrub pa'i phyir ni de yongs su skyob pa'i [#]phyir ro/_/'dod chags dang bral ba'i phyir ni de'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs rnam par 'tshe ba 
[\[220a.4\]]spangs pa'i phyir ro/_/mi dmigs pa'i phyir ni bdag dang gzhan dang snying rjer mi dmigs pa'i phyir ro/_/[#]rnam par dag pa'i phyir ni/_sa brgyad pa la mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob pa'i phyir ro/_/rang gi sems kyi [#]byams la sogs/_/bsgom pa chos la mos pa dang /_/bsam 
[\[220a.5\]]dang dbang 'byor nyid dang ni/_/rnam par mi rtog [#]gcig phyir mchog_/ces bya ba'i don ni gang [#]du bshad pa'i rjes su 'brangs te khong du chud par bya'o/_/de ltar bcom ldan 'das la dang ba rab skyes shing /_/rdzas dang bkur sti chen pos shin tu rgyun mchod ldan/_/yon tan mang ldan phan 
[\[220a.6\]]byed bshes la rtag bsten pa[#]/_/'gro la rjes su brtse ba grub pa kun thob 'gyur/_/'dis ni ji skad bshad pa'i mchod pa dang bstan [#]pa dang tshad med pa rnams kyi [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]dang yon tan mdor bsdus nas ston te/_rdzas dang bkur sti chen pos shin tu rtag tu mchod pa ni rdzas dang bkur sti chen pos 
[\[220a.7\]]shin tu rgyun du mchod pa dang ldan pa ste/_/bkur sti ni yang dag pa'i sgrub pa yin par rig par bya'o/_/de ltar na rnyed pa dang bkur sti dang sgrub pa'i mchod pa dang ldan pa yin no/_/yon tan mang po dang ldan pa ni de las gzhan pa dag dang ngo /_/phan par byed pa ni rjes su brtse bar byed pa nyid 
[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]kyis rig par bya'o/_/grub pa kun thob par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba ni rang dang gzhan gyi don [#]grub pa 'thob par 'gyur ba'o/_/mchod pa dang bstan [#]pa dang tshad med pa'i skabs rdzogs so//_//ngo tsha ba rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad pa bcu drug ste/_/ngo tsha mi mthun phyogs spangs dang /_/
[\[220b.2\]]rnam par mi rtog ye shes ldan/_/kha na ma tho med dman yul/_/sems can smin byed brtan [#]pa'i yin/_/'dis ni ngo bo nyid dang /_grogs dang /_dmigs pa dang /_las phun sum tshogs pas byang chub sems dpa'i ngo tsha ba'i mtshan nyid rnam pa bzhi ston te/[ #]yul kha na ma tho ba med cing dman 
[\[220b.3\]]pa ni nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa ste/_de ni kha na ma tho ba med pa yang yin la/_theg pa chen po bas dman pa yang yin pas des kyang byang chub sems dpa' ngo tsha'o/_/ji ltar sems can rnams yongs su smin par byed ce na/_/ngo tsha ba de nyid la gzhan dag rab tu 'god 
[\[220b.4\]]pa'i phyir ro/_/pha rol [#]phyin pa drug po yi/_/mi mthun phyogs ni 'phel ba dang /_/gnyen po 'grib pas byang chub sems/_/mi bzad ngo tsha skye bar 'gyur/_/'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 'phel ba [#]dang 'grib pas ngo tsha ba yin te/_pha rol tu phyin pa'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs 'phel ba dang /_

[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]to/_/blo ldan ngo tsha med pa ni/_/tshul bzhin min pas nyon mongs dag_/dang du len cing khong khro dang /_/yal bar 'dor dang nga rgyal gyis/_/sems can rnams dang tshul khrims 'joms/_/zhes bya ba 'dis ni bdag nyid la yang gnod par byed la/_gzhan la

[\[223a.5\]]kyi mtshan nyid ni gzhi lnga po 'di dag nyid thos shing 'dzin pa dang /_tshig gis byang ba dang /_yid kyis brtag [#]pa dang /_mthong bas legs par rtogs pa dag yin te/_thos nas [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du de 'dzin pa dang /_kha ton byed pa dang /_ci rigs par de dag gi skyon dang yon tan khong du chud 
[\[223a.6\]]pa'i sgo nas rab tu dang ba'i yid kyis don sems pa dang /_legs par bshad pa dang nyes par bshad pa dmigs phyed pa'i phyir ro/_/mi zad pa nyid ni phung po'i lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa na yang mi zad pa'i phyir ro/_/'bras bu yang dag par 'grub pa ni chos thams cad rnam pa thams cad 
[\[223a.7\]]du mkhyen pa nyid do/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bstan bcos shes pa nyid de yang ting nge [#]'dzin gyi sgo dag dang /_gzungs kyi sgo dag gis bsdus la [#]sems can yongs su smin par byed pa yang yin te/_ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo dag nas de'i bya ba bsgrub [#]pa'i phyir ro/_/dam pa'i chos 'dzin par byed pa 
[\[223b\]]
[\[223b.1\]]yang yin te/_gzungs dag gis de 'dzin pa'i phyir ro/_/'jig rten shes pa nyid kyi tshigs su bcad pa bzhi ste/_brtan rnams 'jig rten shes pa nyid/_/lus dang ngag dang yid dang ni/_/bden pa shes pas mnyam pa med/_/de la gzhan pa dag las 'phags/_/ji ltar lus kyis she na/_bshad 
[\[223b.2\]]pa/_brtan rnams rtag tu 'dzum par byed/_/ji ltar ngag gis she na/_bshad pa/_gsong po dag tu smra ba yin/_/de yang ci'i phyir zhe na/_bshad pa/_sems can snod du bya ba'i phyir/_/don gang gi ched du sems can snod du bya zhe na/_bshad pa/_dam chos bsgrub par bya ba'i phyir/_/
[\[223b.3\]]ji ltar bden pa shes pas zhe na/_bshad pa/_gang phyir bden pa gnyis las ni/_/'jig rten rnam dag 'byung bar 'dod/_/gnyis kyis nub 'gyur de yi [#]phyir/_/de shes 'jig rten shes zhes bya/_/bden pa gnyis kyis ni 'jig rten 'khor bar yang dang yang du 'byung ste/_gang dang gang gis 
[\[223b.4\]]'byung ba'i phyir ro/_/'gog pa dang lam gyi bden pa gnyis kyis ni nub par 'gyur te/_gang yin pa dang /_gang gis nub par 'gyur ba'i phyir ro/_/de lta bas na de shes pa ni 'jig rten shes pa zhes bya ste/_'jig rten 'byung ba dang nub par 'gyur ba shes pa rab dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/de

[\[223b.6\]]pa dang lam gyi bden pa dag go/_/ston pa rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad pa rnam pa gsum ste/_gtsug lag bstan pa'i chos dang ni/_/de don [#]dgongs pa can dang ni/_/nges don tshad ma ldan pa dang /_/de yi brjod med thob pa'o/_/'di ni ston pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid do/_/de la [#]tshad ma 
[\[223b.7\]]dang ldan pa'i don ni ston pa tshad mar gyur pa'am/_des tshad mar [#]mdzad pas nges par byas shing rnam par phye ba gang yin pa'o/_/brjod pa med pa thob pa ni rtogs pa'i ye shes 'jig rten las 'das pa ste[#]/_de ni brjod du med pa'i phyir ro/_/lhag ma ni don go bar zad do/_/spong [#]dang ji skad 
[\[224a\]]
[\[224a.1\]]bshad pa dang /_/log par yang dag shes pa dang /_/brjod dang bcas pa 'thob pa dag_/'dir ni dgag par bstan pa yin/_/ston pa dang po la ni gtsug lag gi chos spong ba'i gang zag dgag par bstan to/_/gnyis pa la ni tshig 'bru sgra ji bzhin gyi don te dgongs 
[\[224a.2\]]pa can gyi don ni ma yin no/_/gsum pa la ni log par bsams pa'i don phyin ci log tu drangs pa'o/_/bzhi pa la ni brjod pa dang bcas pa'i shes pa so so rang gis rig par bya ba ma yin pa'o/_/mos dang rnam par dpyod pa dang /_/gzhan las ji bzhin thos pa dang /_/brjod pa med pa'i ye shes 
[\[224a.3\]]las/_/brtan rnams nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/'di ni ston pa'i phan yon yin te/_ston pa dang pos ni gtsug lag gi [#]chos la mos pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/gnyis pas ni bdag nyid kyi [#]dgongs pa can gyi [#]don dpyod pa las so/_/gsum pas ni gzhan las de'i don gyi tshul phyin

[\[224a.5\]]po mtshungs pa med par 'dod/_/dang po ni rnam grangs shes pa de[#]/_/don re re'i ming gi rnam grangs ji snyed [#]shes pa yin no/_/gnyis pa ni mtshan nyid shes pa ste/_don gang yin pa de'i ming shes pa yin no/_/gsum pa ni tshig shes pa ste/_/yul mi so so'i skad gang dag yin pa shes pa'o/_/

[\[224a.7\]]shes pa kho nas ston/_/chos ni bstan dang bshad phyir dang /_/rnam pa kun du gnyis thob phyir/_/rgol ba rnams kyi lan 'debs phyir/_/so so yang dag rig pa bzhi/_/zhes bya ba ni bzhi nyid kyi rgyu yin no/_/bstan pa la rab tu brtson pa'i gang zhig gang gis ston pa de la ye shes

[\[224b.1\]]dgos so/_/gang zhig ston ce na/_chos dang don no/_/gang gis ston ce na/_tshig gi [#]shes pas so/_/de la chos dang don dag ston pa ni chos bstan pa dang bshad pa'i phyir ro/_/tshig gis ston pa ni gnyis po de nyid rnam pa thams cad du thob par byed pa'i phyir ro/_/shes pas ston 
[\[224b.2\]]pa ni rgol ba rnams kyi [#]lan 'debs pa'i phyir ro/_/de bas na gang zhig gang gis ston pa de shes pa'i phyir/_so so yang dag par rig pa bzhir rnam par gzhag go/_/mnyam nyid so so rang rig nas/_/phyis ni rab tu shes pa yin/_/the tshom thams cad gcod byed pa/_/so so yang dag rig par brjod/_/'dis ni so

[\[224b.5\]]gcig gis 'khor bar mngon mtho byed/_/gzhan gyis nyon mongs med par 'khor/_/'dis ni tshogs gang yin pa dang gang gi don du yin pa de bstan to/_/tshogs ni rnam pa gnyis te/_/bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /_ye shes kyi tshogs so/_/de la bsod nams kyi tshogs

[\[225a.1\]]rab tu yongs su bsngos pa'i sgo nas ye shes kyi tshogs yin par rig par bya'o/_/rgyun du sgom pa thob nas ni/_/dge la yang dang yang du sgrub/_/brtan [#]pa'i tshogs gang yin pa de/_/don kun sgrub par byed pa yin/_/'di ni tshogs kyi [#]nges pa'i tshig dang las [#]yin te/_/sam

[\[225b.1\]]la sogs pa'i chos la bdag med par 'jug pa'i sgo nas phyin ci [#]log bzhi'i gnyen po mi gtsang ba dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_mi rtag pa dang /_bdag med pa'i 'du shes rnams kyi [#]yang gnyen po yin pa'i phyir ro/_/'jug pas ji lta bu zhe na/_dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhis go

[\[226a.1\]]na/_bsgom [#]pa yongs su grub pa rnams 'dres pa nye bar 'dres pa lhun gyis grub par bsgom [#]pa'i phyir ro/_/bsgoms pas ji lta bu zhe na/_shin tu bsgoms pas phung po'i lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa na yang de mi zad pa'i phyir ro/_/yang dag par 'grub pas ji

[\[226b.1\]]pa/_/yang dag spong ba thams cad la/_/gnyen po zhes ni nges par brjod/_/'di ni 'dun pa bskyed do/_/'bad do/_/brtson 'grus rtsom [#]mo/_/sems rab tu 'dzin to/_/yang dag par rab tu 'jog go zhes bya ba'i tshig 'di rnams kyi don bstan te/_'dun pa la brten nas zhi gnas dang lhag

[\[227a.1\]]phan 'dogs bdag nyid yin/_/gsum pa gtod par byed pa ste/_/bzhi pa gnyen po pa yin no/_/spong ba'i 'du byed brgyad las 'dun pa dang rtsol ba dang dad pa ni 'bad par byed pa'i thabs te/_dad cing don du gnyer na 'bad pa'i phyir ro/_/shin tu sbyangs pa ni phan

[\[227b.1\]]ni shes bya ste/_/don grub byed pa'i skabs kyis so/_/dad pa'i dbang po'i gzhi ni byang chub ste dmigs pa zhes bya ba'i don to/_/brtson 'grus kyi dbang po'i gzhi [#]ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'o/_/dran pa'i dbang po'i gzhi [#]ni theg pa chen pos bsdus pa'i thos pa'o/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi

[\[241a.4\]]de/_khong na 'dzin par byed/_skyed par byed/_snun par byed/_gso bar byed/_kun tu skyed par byed/_ngam grog las kyang srung bar byed/_smra ba la yang slob par byed do/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi las lnga po 'di dag kyang de dang chos mthun par rig par bya ste/_
[\[241a.5\]]'phags pa'i sa ni 'phags pa'i chos yin par rig par bya'o[#]/_/sems can kun la rtag par ni/_/dad pa skyed par byed pa dang /_/lhag pa'i khrims sogs slob pa dang /_/rnam par grol la sbyor ba'i phyir/_/sangs rgyas la ni gsol ba dang /_/de dag sgrib pa sbyong ba'i

[\[241b\]]
[\[241b.1\]]pa yin no/_/chung ma ni rnam par grol ba ste/_rnam par grol ba'i dga' ba dang bde ba yang dag par myong ba'i phyir ro/_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen dag ni sangs rgyas rnams so/_/chags nyen lta bu ni sgrib pa'o/_/gang dag bstan par mi 'os pa/_/sems can rnams la gsang 
[\[241b.2\]]byed cing /_/bslab las nyams la smod pa dang /_/phun sum tshogs pa bsngags pa nyid/_/gdams ngag sbyin par byed pa dang /_/bdud rnams sbron par byed pa ste/_/las lnga dag gis rgyal sras rnams/_/sems can rtsa lag 'dra ba yin/_/rtsa lag dag ni rtsa lag rnams la phan pa rnam 
[\[241b.3\]]pa lnga byed de/_gsang bar bya ba gsang bar byed/_spyod pa ngan pa la smod par byed/_spyod pa bzang po la bsngags par byed/_bya ba rnams la grogs kyi ngo bor 'gro bar byed/_nyam nga ba'i gnas dag las zlog par byed do/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi las lnga po 'di dag kyang 
[\[241b.4\]]de dang chos mthun par rig par bya ste/_'os pa ma yin pa dag la zab mo bshad pa [#]gsang ba'i phyir dang /_bslab pa nyams pa dang /_phun sum tshogs pa dag la [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du smod pa dang bsngags pa'i phyir dang /_rtogs par bya ba'i ched du 'doms pa'i phyir dang /_bdud

[\[241b.6\]]rnams/_/sems can grogs po 'dra ba yin/_/grogs po'i phan pa dang bde ba la [#]phyin ci ma log pa dang /_bde ba dang phan pa nye bar sgrub pa dang /_mi phyed par 'gyur ba dang /_rtag tu phan pa dang bde ba 'dod pa gang yin pa de ni grogs po yin te/_de bzhin du byang chub sems

[\[242a.6\]]po 'di dag kyang de dang chos mthun par rig par bya'o/_/sems can don la shin tu brtson/_/tshogs rnams rdzogs par byed pa dang /_/tshogs bsags myur du grol bar byed/_/mi mthun phyogs ni spong byed cing /_/'jig rten 'jig rten ma yin pa'i/_/phun sum sna tshogs

[\[243b\]]
[\[243b.1\]]can rnams kyi mi mthun phyogs/_/rnam pa drug ni 'dul bar byed/_/pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi mi mthun pa'i phyogs ni rnam pa drug ste/_ser sna dang /_'chal ba'i tshul khrims dang /_khong [#]khro ba dang /_le lo dang /_rnam par g.yeng ba dang /_'chal ba'i shes rab dag dang /_go

[\[243b.3\]]pa yis/_/nga rgyal rtsol ba spangs pa dang /_/sangs rgyas rgyal sras thams cad dang /_/ngo bo gcig tu gyur pa las/_/yang de zhing dang ming dang ni/_/dus dang bskal pa'i ming dang ni/_/'khor dang dam pa'i chos dag kyang /_/gnas pa'i sgo [#]nas der 'dod do/_/de la gang zag gi bye brag

[\[243b.7\]]'di zhes bya ba la sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di lta bu zhig tu sangs rgyas mtshan 'di zhes bya bar 'gyur te/_'di'i 'khor ni 'di lta bur 'gyur ro/_/'di'i dam pa'i chos ni dus 'di srid cig tu gnas par 'gyur ro zhes bya bar ro/_/nges par 'gyur ba'i rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad

[\[244a.4\]]dang /_sems can gyi bya ba sgrub pa'i phyir ro/_/lhun gyis grub par mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob par nges par 'gyur ba ni/_rtag tu lhun gyis grub par rnam par mi rtog pa'i ye shes kyis gnas pa'i phyir ro/_/gdon mi za bar bya [#]dgos pa rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad pa rtsa ba drug 
[\[244a.5\]]ste/_mchod bslab yang dag blang ba dang /_/snying rje dge ba bsgom [#]pa dang /_/de bzhin dgon par bag yod dang /_/thos don ngoms mi shes pa nyid/_/sa rnams kun la brtan pa yi/_/gdon mi za bar bya ba nyid/_/gdon mi za bar bya dgos pa 'di drug ni/_pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi [#]dbang

[\[244b.1\]]chos sbyin tshul khrims dag pa dang /_/mi skye bzod pa thob nyid dang /_theg chen brtson 'grus rtsom pa dang /_/mtha' la snying rje ldan gnas dang /_/shes rab blo dang ldan rnams kyi/_/pha rol phyin pa'i [#]gtso bor 'dod/_/rnam pa 'di drug ni pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi gtso bo yin te/_de [#]la tshul

[\[245a.1\]]pa gdags pa rnam par gzhag pa ni rnam pa bzhi ste/_ltos pa'i rigs pa dang /_bya ba byed pa'i rigs pa dang /_'thad pa sgrub pa'i rigs pa dang /_chos nyid kyi rigs pa'o/_/theg pa gdags pa rnam par gzhag pa ni rnam pa gsum ste/_nyan thos kyi

[\[245b\]]
[\[245b.1\]]pa yongs su tshol ba'o/_/de la dngos po la ming glo bur ba nyid du yongs su tshol ba ni ming yongs su tshol ba yin par rig par bya'o/_/ming la dngos po glo bur ba nyid du yongs su tshol ba ni dngos po yongs su tshol ba yin par rig par bya'o/_/de gnyis 'brel ba la ngo bo nyid dang khyad

[\[259a.7\]]pa'i dus la de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes ma chags ma thogs pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_da ltar byung ba'i dus la de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes ma chags ma thogs pa zhes bya ba ni ye shes kyis bsdus pa gsum yin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i phrin las thams 
[\[259b\]]
[\[259b.1\]]cad dang /_gsung gi phrin las thams cad dang /_/thugs kyi phrin las thams cad ye shes sngon du 'gro zhing ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang ba [#]zhes bya ba ni/_phrin las kyis bsdus pa gsum yin no/_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad 
[\[259b.2\]]pa/_sku gsum dag gi [#]byang chub che/_/rnam pa kun brnyes thams cad du/_/sems can kun gyi the tshom dag_/gcod pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'dis ni sku gsum gyi [#]rnam pa thams cad byang chub pa brnyes pa'i phyir dang /_/shes bya thams cad rnam pa 
[\[259b.3\]]thams cad du mkhyen pa'i phyir/_bcom ldan 'das kyi rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su bstan te/_sku gsum ni ngo bo nyid med pa pa [#]dang /_longs spyod rdzogs pa pa dang /_sprul pa pa'o/_/shes bya thams cad kyi rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa [#]ni/_'dir 
[\[259b.4\]]lha dang mi la sogs pa'i sems can thams cad kyi the tshom thams cad gcod pa'i phrin las kyis bstan to/_/pha rol tu phyin pa drug [#]yongs su rdzogs pa'i rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad pa/_'dzin pa med cing nyes pa med/_/rnyog pa med cing mi gnas pa/_/
[\[259b.5\]]mi g.yo chos rnams thams cad la/_/spros [#]med khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'dis ni pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi mi mthun pa'i phyogs mtha' dag las nges par grol bas/_bcom ldan 'das kyi [#]pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs par brjod de[#]/_de 
[\[259b.6\]]la 'dzin pa nyid ni/_longs spyod la kun tu 'dzin pa med pa'i phyir rig par bya'o/_/nyes pa med pa nyid ni[#]/_lus la sogs pa'i las dri ma med pa'i phyir ro/_/rnyog pa [#]med pa ni/_'jig rten gyi chos dang /_sdug bsngal dag gis sems rnyog pa can du 
[\[259b.7\]]mi byed pa'i phyir ro/_/mi gnas pa nyid ni rtogs pa chung ngu dang /[ (]dan [,]ngan [)]ngon tsam la mi gnas pa'i phyir ro/_/mi g.yo ba [#]nyid ni/_/rnam par mi g.yeng ba'i phyir ro/_/spros [#]pa med pa nyid ni/_rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa thams cad kun tu mi spyod pa'i phyir 
[\[260a\]]
[\[260a.1\]]ro/_/sangs rgyas kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dbye ba'i tshigs su bcad pa gnyis te/_dam pa'i don ni grub pa ste/_/sa kun las ni nges par byung /_/sems can kun gyi mchog tu gyur/_/sems can thams cad rnam grol mdzad/_/yon tan mi 
[\[260a.2\]]zad mnyam med ldan/_/'jig rten dag dang dkyil 'khor na'ang /_/snang la lha dang mi rnams kyis/_/rnam pa kun tu mi mthong ngo /_/'dir ngo bo nyid dang /_rgyu dang /_'bras bu dang /_las dang ldan pa dang /_'jug pa'i don drug gis sangs rgyas kyi mtshan nyid 
[\[260a.3\]]yongs su bstan te/_de la don dam pa grub pa ni/_de bzhin nyid rnam par dag pa ste/_de ni sangs rgyas rnams [#]kyi ngo bo nyid kyi sku'o/_/byang chub sems dpa'i sa thams cad las nges par byung ba nyid ni rgyu'o/_/sems can thams cad kyi mchog tu gyur pa nyid ni 
[\[260a.4\]]'bras bu'o/_/sems can thams cad rnam par grol bar mdzad pa nyid ni las so/_/yon tan mi zad pa dang /_mnyam pa med pa dang ldan pa nyid ni ldan pa'o/_/'jig rten gyi khams sna tshogs su snang ba nyid ni/_sprul pa'i skus so/_/'khor gyi [#]
[\[260a.5\]]dkyil 'khor rnams na'ang snang ba nyid ni/_longs spyod rdzogs pa'i skus so/_/rnam pa thams cad du mi snang ba nyid ni chos kyi skus te/_'jug pa'i rab tu dbye ba rnam pa gsum yin no/_/[#]theg pa chen po [#]mdo sde'i rgyan byang chub sems dpa' chen po rtogs pa rnam 
[\[260a.6\]]par byang bas bshad pa las/_spyod pa dang mthar thug [#]pa'i skabs zhes bya ba ste/_le'u nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'o//_//theg pa chen po [#]mdo sde'i rgyan gyi bshad pa rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po shAkya sing+ha dang /_zhu chen gyi lo tsA ba ban+de 
[\[260a.7\]]dpal brtsegs la sogs pas bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa//_//theg chen mdo sde kun gyi rgyan/_/bshad pa 'di'i gzhung tshad ni/_/sha [#]lo ka sum stong dag dang rtsa/_/drug brgya lnga bcu yin par bsgrags//_//[#]
